# Brain Activation Analysis (VOXI Format)

## Reference Atlas
```voxi
V 91 109 91
-- Human Brain Atlas (MNI152 2mm space)
-- Color = lobe: blue=frontal, green=temporal, magenta=parietal,
--   red=occipital, gray=subcortical, yellow=cingulate,
--   orange=insular, cyan=cerebellum
-- === FRONTAL ===
-- L-DLPFC: working memory, executive control
-- R-DLPFC: working memory, executive control
-- mPFC: self-reference, default mode
-- ACC: conflict monitoring, error detection
-- L-IFG: speech production (Broca's), syntax
-- R-IFG: inhibition, prosody
-- SMA: motor planning, sequencing
-- L-premotor: motor preparation
-- R-premotor: motor preparation
-- L-OFC: reward, decision-making
-- R-OFC: reward, decision-making
-- === TEMPORAL ===
-- L-fusiform: face perception (FFA), visual word form
-- R-fusiform: face perception (FFA)
-- L-hippocampus: memory encoding, spatial navigation
-- R-hippocampus: memory encoding, spatial navigation
-- L-STG: auditory processing, speech perception
-- R-STG: auditory processing, voice
-- L-MTG: semantic processing, language
-- R-MTG: semantic processing
-- L-STS: biological motion, social cues
-- R-STS: biological motion, social cues
-- L-temporal-pole: social concepts, semantic memory
-- R-temporal-pole: social concepts, semantic memory
-- === PARIETAL ===
-- L-IPL: attention, tool use, number
-- R-IPL: attention, spatial awareness
-- L-SPL: visuospatial, reaching
-- R-SPL: visuospatial, reaching
-- precuneus: self-awareness, episodic memory
-- L-SMG: phonological processing
-- R-SMG: spatial attention
-- L-AG: semantic integration, reading
-- R-AG: spatial cognition
-- === OCCIPITAL ===
-- V1: primary visual, edges/orientation
-- L-V2/V3: early visual processing
-- R-V2/V3: early visual processing
-- L-V4: color, form perception
-- R-V4: color, form perception
-- L-V5/MT: motion perception
-- R-V5/MT: motion perception
-- L-LOC: object recognition
-- R-LOC: object recognition
-- === SUBCORTICAL ===
-- L-thalamus: sensory relay, attention gating
-- R-thalamus: sensory relay, attention gating
-- L-amygdala: emotion, threat detection, salience
-- R-amygdala: emotion, threat detection, salience
-- L-caudate: learning, habit formation
-- R-caudate: learning, habit formation
-- L-putamen: motor control, reward learning
-- R-putamen: motor control, reward learning
-- L-NAcc: reward, motivation
-- R-NAcc: reward, motivation
-- === CINGULATE ===
-- PCC: default mode, self-reflection
-- dACC: cognitive control, pain
-- mid-cingulate: salience, motor
-- === INSULAR ===
-- L-insula: interoception, empathy, disgust
-- R-insula: interoception, empathy, disgust
-- === CEREBELLUM ===
-- L-cerebellum: motor coordination, timing
-- R-cerebellum: motor coordination, timing
-- vermis: balance, posture
```

## Activation Data
```voxi
V 46 53 63
-- Visual processing vs auditory processing
-- Threshold: |t| >= 3.0
-- 11 significant clusters

-- REGION FUNCTIONS:
--   L-cerebellum: motor coordination, timing
--   L-SPL: visuospatial, reaching
--   L-amygdala: emotion, threat detection, salience
--   R-fusiform: face perception (FFA)
--   ACC: conflict monitoring, error detection
--   SMA: motor planning, sequencing
--   R-premotor: motor preparation

-- cluster-1: L-cerebellum, 1021 vox, peak t=11.4, MNI (-30,-78,-6)
E 16 15 11 9 7 8 4
-- cluster-2: L-cerebellum, 967 vox, peak t=9.7, MNI (-27,-78,57)
E 17 36 11 11 7 7 4
-- cluster-3: L-SPL, 652 vox, peak t=-12.1, MNI (-27,-15,84)
E 17 45 32 5 5 8 5
-- cluster-4: L-amygdala, 529 vox, peak t=-9.6, MNI (-21,-15,-27)
E 19 8 32 5 6 8 9
-- cluster-5: unknown, 96 vox, peak t=7.3, MNI (21,-51,57)
S 33 36 20 3 8
-- cluster-6: R-fusiform, 64 vox, peak t=5.9, MNI (27,-51,-12)
E 35 13 20 4 2 2 2
-- cluster-7: ACC, 54 vox, peak t=5.7, MNI (12,0,-24)
E 30 9 37 3 2 2 3
-- cluster-8: SMA, 43 vox, peak t=5.5, MNI (0,9,75)
S 26 42 40 2 3
-- cluster-9: SMA, 32 vox, peak t=5.1, MNI (18,0,81)
E 32 44 37 3 1 2 3
-- cluster-10: R-premotor, 31 vox, peak t=5.2, MNI (24,0,57)
E 34 36 37 3 2 2 3
-- cluster-11: ACC, 13 vox, peak t=4.2, MNI (0,15,-12)
E 26 13 42 1 2 2 3
```
